####################################
# AI events
####################################

#Releasing puppets

event = {
	id = 2400002
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {	
		ai = yes
		local_flag = AllInit
		NOT = { local_flag = AllInitCommie }
		government = communist
		atwar = no
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"	
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = AllInitNonCommie }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = AllInitCommie }
		command = { type = ai which = "release_communist.ai" }
	}
}

	id = 2400003

		command = { type = local_clrflag which = AllInitCommie }

	}
}

event = {
	id = 2400004
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {

		OR = { 
			local_flag = AllInitCommie

		}

		command = { type = ai which = "release_none.ai" }
	}
}


#Unpuppeting

event = {
	id = 2400005
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {	
		ai = yes
		atwar = no
		local_flag = AllInit
		NOT = { local_flag = AIfreeCommie }
		government = communist
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"	
	
	#date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1940 }
	#offset = 30
	#deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = AIfreeNonCommie }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = AIfreeCommie }
		command = { type = ai which = "releaseNation_communist.ai" }
	}
}

event = {
	id = 2400007
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {

###################################
# Minor invasion shut down events
###################################

event = {
	id = 2400030
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = yes
		local_flag = AllInit
		NOT = { local_flag = InvShutOff }
		NOT = {
			country = GER
			country = U08
			country = ITA
			country = RUS
			country = USA
			country = VIC
			country = ENG
			country = JAP
			country = SOV
			country = CHI
			country = SPR
			country = SPA
		}
		OR = {
			alliance = { country = GER }
			alliance = { country = U08 }
			alliance = { country = ITA }
			alliance = { country = RUS }
			alliance = { country = USA }
			alliance = { country = VIC }
			alliance = { country = ENG }
			alliance = { country = JAP }
			alliance = { country = SOV }
			alliance = { country = CHI }
			alliance = { country = SPR }
			alliance = { country = SPA }
			
			country = FIN
		}
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	style = 0	
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1913 }
	offset = 5
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }
	
	action = {
		command = { type = local_setflag which = InvShutOff }
		command = { type = ai which = "invasionNo.ai" }
	}
}

###############################################
# ai_mobi events
###############################################

event = {
	id = 2400034
	random = no
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {
		local_flag = AllInit
		ai = yes
		OR = {
			atwar = yes
			policy = { type = professional_army value = 5 }
			local_flag = demilitarized
			lost_VP	= { value = 1 }
		}
		NOT = { local_flag = reinf }
	}
   
	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1908 }
	offset = 2
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = noreinf }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = reinf }
		command = { type = ai which = "Reinforcement_Boost_Up.ai" }
	}
}	

event = {
	id = 2400035
	random = no
	persistent = yes

	trigger = {
		local_flag = AllInit
		ai = yes
		NOT = {
			atwar = yes
			policy = { type = professional_army value = 5 }
			local_flag = demilitarized
			NOT = { lost_VP	= { value = 1 } }
		}
		NOT = { local_flag = noreinf }
	}
   
	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1908 }
	offset = 2
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = reinf }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = noreinf }
		command = { type = ai which = "Reinforcement_Boost_Down.ai" }
	}	
}

# morale collapses/recovers events
###############################################


event = {
	id = 2400151
	persistent = yes
	
	name = EVT_2400151_NAME
	desc = EVT_2400151_DESC
	style = 2
	
	action = {
		name = ACTION_NAME_DAMN
		command = { type = max_organization which = land value = -16 }
		command = { type = morale 			which = land value = -8  }
		command = { type = ground_def_eff   when = 1  value = -0.08  }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = disOrgGeneric_1 when = 1 value = 1 }
	}
}

event = {
	id = 2400160
	persistent = yes
	
	name = EVT_2400160_NAME
	desc = EVT_2400160_DESC
	style = 2
	
	action = {
		name = "ACTION_NAME_GREAT"
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				local_flag = DisOrg1
				local_flag = DisOrg2
				local_flag = DisOrg3
				local_flag = disOrgGeneric_1
				local_flag = disOrgGeneric_2
				local_flag = disOrgGeneric_3
				local_flag = disOrgGeneric_4
			}
		}
		
		#maxORG=50%
		#maxMor=35%
		#maxGDE=25%
		
		#after 4:
		#ORG=-40%
		#Mor=-32%
		#GDE=-16%
		
		#full DisOrg
		#ORG=-30%
		#Mor=-30%
		#GDE=-10%
		
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = disOrgGeneric_1 when = 1 value = 1 } } type = max_organization which = land value = 16 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = disOrgGeneric_1 when = 1 value = 1 } } type = morale 		  which = land value = 8  }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = disOrgGeneric_1 when = 1 value = 1 } } type = ground_def_eff   when = 1  value = 0.08  }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = disOrgGeneric_1 when = 1 value = 1 } } type = local_setflag which = disOrgGeneric_1 when = 1 value = -1 }
		
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = disOrgGeneric_1 when = 1 value = 1 } } type = max_organization which = land value = 16 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = disOrgGeneric_1 when = 1 value = 1 } } type = morale 		  which = land value = 8  }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = disOrgGeneric_1 when = 1 value = 1 } } type = ground_def_eff   when = 1  value = 0.08  }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = disOrgGeneric_1 when = 1 value = 1 } } type = local_setflag which = disOrgGeneric_1 when = 1 value = -1 }
		
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = disOrgGeneric_1 when = 1 value = 1 } } type = max_organization which = land value = 16 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = disOrgGeneric_1 when = 1 value = 1 } } type = morale 		  which = land value = 8  }

###############################################
# Nuke AI events
###############################################

event = {
	id = 2400070
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {	
		ai = yes
		local_flag = AllInit
		ic = 50
		NOT = { ispuppet = yes }
		#AI never builds more than 2 at a time
		OR = {
			AND = {
				technology = 5520 #max-size = 3
				NOT = { nuclear_reactor = 1 }
				NOT = { local_flag = nukeBuild }
			}
			AND = {
				technology = 5530 #max-size = 4
				NOT = { nuclear_reactor = 2 }
				NOT = { local_flag = nukeBuild }
			}
			AND = {
				technology = 5540 #max-size = 5
				NOT = { nuclear_reactor = 3 }
				NOT = { local_flag = nukeBuild }
			}
			AND = {
				technology = 7170 #max-size = 6
				NOT = { nuclear_reactor = 4 }
				NOT = { local_flag = nukeBuild }
			}
			AND = {
				technology = 7180 #max-size = 7
				NOT = { nuclear_reactor = 5 }
				NOT = { local_flag = nukeBuild }
			}
			AND = {
				technology = 7190 #max-size = 8
				NOT = { nuclear_reactor = 6 }
				NOT = { local_flag = nukeBuild }
			}
			AND = {
				technology = 16010 #max-size = 9
				NOT = { nuclear_reactor = 7 }
				NOT = { local_flag = nukeBuild }
			}
			AND = {
				technology = 16020 #max-size = 10
				NOT = { nuclear_reactor = 8 }
				NOT = { local_flag = nukeBuild }
			}
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { country = USA country = VIC country = JAP country = SOV country = GER country = ENG }
			AND = {
				country = USA
				control = { province = 6093 data = -1 }
			}
			AND = {
				country = VIC
				control = { province = 3139 data = -1 }
			}
			AND = {
				country = JAP
				control = { province = 3071 data = -1 }
			}
			AND = {
				country = SOV
				control = { province = 7158 data = -1 }
			}
			AND = {
				country = GER
				control = { province = 2597 data = -1 }
			}
			AND = {
				country = ENG
				control = { province = 2245 data = -1 }
			}
		}
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"	
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = local_setflag which = builtNuclearReactors when = 1 value = 1 }
		command = { type = manpowerpool value = -0.5 }
		command = { type = free_ic value = -15 } #15 instead of 20 because free_ic subtracts base IC rather than effective IC
		command = { type = event which = 2400071 where = -1 when = 166 }
		
		command = { type = local_setflag which = nukeBuild }
	}
}

event = {
	id = 2400071
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	
	name = "AI_EVENT"	
	
	action = {
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = nukeBuild }
		command = { type = free_ic value = 15 }
		command = { trigger = { nuclear_reactor = 1 } type = construct which = nuclear_reactor where = -4 value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { nuclear_reactor = 1 } NOT = { country = USA country = VIC country = JAP country = SOV country = GER country = ENG } } type = construct which = nuclear_reactor where = -1 value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { control = { province = 6093 data = -1 } NOT = { nuclear_reactor = 1 } country = USA }	type = construct which = nuclear_reactor where = 6093 	value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { control = { province = 3139 data = -1 } NOT = { nuclear_reactor = 1 } country = VIC } 	type = construct which = nuclear_reactor where = 3139 	value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { control = { province = 3071 data = -1 } NOT = { nuclear_reactor = 1 } country = JAP } 	type = construct which = nuclear_reactor where = 3071 	value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { control = { province = 3699 data = -1 } NOT = { nuclear_reactor = 1 } country = SOV } 	type = construct which = nuclear_reactor where = 3699 	value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { control = { province = 2597 data = -1 } NOT = { nuclear_reactor = 1 } country = GER } 	type = construct which = nuclear_reactor where = 2597 	value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { control = { province = 2245 data = -1 } NOT = { nuclear_reactor = 1 } country = ENG } 	type = construct which = nuclear_reactor where = 2245 	value = 1 }
		#if lost control of the province, money is wasted.
	}
}

#General nuke warnings. Also has additional AI logic for temporarily disabling nukes if Germany is very close to defeat

event = {
	id = 2400072
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {	
		NOT = { local_flag = nukeWarning }
		nuke = 1
	}

	name = EVT_2400072_NAME
	desc = EVT_2400072_DESC	
	style = 2
	picture = "nuke"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 1
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = "ACTION_NAME_GREAT"
		command = { type = enable_task		which = nuke 				}
		command = { type = local_setflag 	which = nukeWarning 		}
		command = { type = local_setflag 	which = nukeW_selfTrigger	}
		command = { type = trigger 	which = 2400073 } 
		command = { type = trigger 	which = 2400074 } 
		command = { type = event 	which = 2400075 where = -1 when = 5 } #self-trigger clear
		
		command = { trigger = {	
						NOT = {
							war = { country = GER }
							war = { country = JAP }
							war = { country = ITA }
							war = { country = ENG }
							war = { country = VIC }
							war = { country = SOV }
							war = { country = USA }
							nuked = -1
						}
					}
					type = local_setflag 	which = nukeDisable 		}
					
		command = { trigger = {	
						ai = yes
						local_flag = AllInit
						war = { country = GER }
						NOT = { 
							nuked = -1	# True if against that country has been used at least one nuke
							#nuked = 1	# True if that country has used at least one nuke against any country
							nuked = { country = GER  data = -1 } #Ger has been nuked by someone else
							lost_national = { value = 5 }
						}
						lost_national = { country = GER value = 40 } #Germany is losing bad
					}
					type = local_setflag 	which = nukeDisableAI 		}
					
		command = { trigger = {	OR = { local_flag = nukeDisable local_flag = nukeDisableAI } }
					type = disable_task		which = nuke 				}
					
		command = { trigger = {	local_flag = nukeDisableAI }
					type = event which = 2400076 where = -1 when = 120	} #giving Germany 4 months to surrender
	}
}

#reenable nukes early if player took over or got nuked or other conditions changed

event = {
	id = 2400077
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {	
		local_flag = nukeDisableAI
		NOT = {
			AND = {
				ai = yes
				local_flag = AllInit
				war = { country = GER }
				NOT = { 
					nuked = -1	# True if against that country has been used at least one nuke
					#nuked = 1	# True if that country has used at least one nuke against any country
					nuked = { country = GER  data = -1 } #Ger has been nuked by someone else
					nuked = { country = GER  data =  1 } #Ger has used nukes at least once
					lost_national = { value = 5 }
				}
				lost_national = { country = GER value = 20 } #Germany is losing bad
			}
		}
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"		
	style = 2
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { local_flag = nukeDisable } }  type = enable_task		which = nuke }
		command = { type = local_clrflag 	which = nukeDisableAI }
	}
}

	action = {
		name = "ACTION_NAME_OK"
		command = { type = enable_task		which = nuke 		}
		command = { type = local_clrflag 	which = nukeDisable }
	}
}

		trigger = { ai = no }
		command = { type = belligerence 	value = 100 }
		command = { type = dissent 			value = 30  }
		command = { type = local_setflag 	which = nukePenalty }
		command = { type = leave_alliance 	when  = 0   }
	}
	action = {
		name = "AI_EVENT"
		trigger = { ai = yes } #Can't stop AI from using nukes recklessly, so can't penalize him as much either
		command = { type = belligerence 	value = 10  }
		command = { type = dissent 			value = 3   }
		command = { type = local_setflag 	which = nukePenalty }
	}
}


########################################################################
# garrison proportion decreases as territory is lost, default for most countries is 30
# garrison includes reserves located directly behind the frontline
# unlike lost_national, lost_VP will work on colonies as well although it is less precise
########################################################################

event = {
	id = 2400090
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {	
		ai = yes
		local_flag = AllInit
		local_flag = gar #variable is set
		NOT = { local_flag = { which = gar when = 0 value = 30 } }
		NOT = { local_flag = garSpec }
		
		NOT = { army = 300 }
		NOT = { lost_VP = { value = 3 } }
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = local_setflag which = gar value = 30 }
		command = { type = ai which = "garrison30.ai" }
	}
}

event = {
	id = 2400091
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {	
		ai = yes
		local_flag = AllInit
		NOT = { local_flag = { which = gar when = 0 value = 25 } }
		NOT = { local_flag = garSpec }
		
		OR = {
			AND = {
				army = 300
				NOT = { army = 600 }
			}
			AND = {
				lost_VP = { value = 3 }
				NOT = { lost_VP = { value = 15 } }
			}
		}
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = local_setflag which = gar value = 25 }
		command = { type = ai which = "garrison25.ai" }
	}
}

				NOT = { lost_VP = { value = 30 } }
			}

	offset = 15

	random = no

	trigger = {	
		ai = yes

				army = 900
				NOT = { army = 1200 }
			}
			AND = {
				lost_VP = { value = 30 } #SOV at top
				NOT = { lost_VP = { value = 45 } }
			}
		}
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

event = {
	id = 2400094
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {	
		ai = yes
		local_flag = AllInit
		NOT = { local_flag = { which = gar when = 0 value = 10 } }
		NOT = { local_flag = garSpec }
		
		OR = {
			AND = {
				army = 1200
				NOT = { army = 1500 }
			}
			AND = {
				lost_VP = { value = 45 }
				NOT = { lost_VP = { value = 60 } }

	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {	
		ai = yes
		local_flag = AllInit
		OR = {
			nuclear_reactor = 4 #building nuclear reactors
			nuke = 1 #residual nukes, maybe reactor was destroyed
		}
		NOT = { strategic_bomber = { value = 2 } }
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"	
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = local_setflag which = builtStratBombers when = 1 value = 1 }
		command = { type = build_division which = strategic_bomber value = escort when = 2 cost = -1 where = 200 }	
	}
}

		NOT = { paratrooper = { value = 2 } }

	}

	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = local_setflag which = builtParatroopers when = 1 value = 1 }
		command = { 															type = build_division which = paratrooper 		when = 2 cost = 12 where = 180 }
		command = { trigger = { technology = 2050 technology = 2330 } 			type = build_brigade  value = glider_armor 		when = 1 cost = 6  where = 65  }
		command = { trigger = { technology = 2050 technology = 2330 } 			type = build_brigade  value = glider_artillery 	when = 1 cost = 6  where = 60  }
		command = { trigger = { technology = 2050 NOT = { technology = 2330 } } type = build_brigade  value = glider_armor 		when = 2 cost = 6  where = 65  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { technology = 2050 } technology = 2330 } type = build_brigade  value = glider_artillery 	when = 2 cost = 6  where = 60  }
	}
}

#### Build Convoys
event = {
	id = 2400173
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {	
		ai = yes
		local_flag = AllInit
		NOT = { local_flag = buildingConvoys }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				local_flag = AI1936_naval
				OR = {
					NOT = { escortpool = 300  }
					NOT = { convoypool = 1500 }
				}
			}
			AND = {
				local_flag = AI1936_mixed
				OR = {
					NOT = { escortpool = 60  }
					NOT = { convoypool = 300 }
				}
			}
		}
		supplies = 8000
		IC = 50
		NOT = { lost_national = { value = 5 } }
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"	
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = local_setflag which = buildingConvoys }
		#No way to initiate real convoy build
		#10 Convoy - 2.4 IC * 75 days * 4 Sp/IC * 10 = 7200 (0   manpower)
		#10 Escort - 2.4 IC * 80 days * 4 Sp/IC * 2  = 1536 (0.3 manpower)
		
		command = { type = supplies 	value = -8736 }
		command = { type = manpowerpool value = -0.3  }
		
		command = { type = event which = 2400174 where = -1 when = 30 } 
	}
}

event = {
	id = 2400174
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	
	name = "AI_EVENT"	

	action = {
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = buildingConvoys }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = builtConvoys when = 1 value = 1 }
		
		command = { type = transport_pool 	which = -1 value = 100 } 
		command = { type = escort_pool 		which = -1 value = 20  } 
	}
}

#### Build TPs
event = {
	id = 2400175
	persistent = yes
	random = no

	trigger = { 
		ai = yes
		local_flag = AllInit
		manpower = 1
		OR = {
			atwar 	= yes
			random	= 10
		}
		#counts build queue as well
		OR = {
			AND = {
				navy = 1
				NOT = { transport = { value = 1 } }
				atwar = no
			}
			AND = {
				navy = 10
				NOT = { transport = { value = 1 } }
			}
			AND = {
				navy = 50
				NOT = { transport = { value = 2 } }
			}
			AND = {
				navy = 100
				NOT = { transport = { value = 3 } }
			}
			AND = {
				local_flag = AI1936_mixed
				NOT = { transport = { value = 6 } }
			}
			AND = {
				local_flag = AI1936_naval
				NOT = { transport = { value = 12 } }
			}
		}
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"	

	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1914 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 30 month = december year = 1999 }
	
	action = {
		command = { type = local_setflag which = builtTPs when = 1 value = 1 }
		command = { type = build_division which = transport when = 2 cost = -1 where = 90 }
	}
}

#Override default build
event = {
	id = 2400181
	random = no 
	tag = { U87 FIN CHC }
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {	
		ai = yes
		local_flag = AllInit
		local_flag = AI1936_land #any
		NOT = { local_flag = buildSpec }
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { trigger = { country = U87 } type = ai which = "nations\U87.ai" }
		command = { trigger = { country = FIN } type = ai which = "nations\FIN.ai" }
		command = { trigger = { country = CHC } type = ai which = "nations\CHC.ai" }
		command = { trigger = { country = MEN } type = ai which = "nations\MEN.ai" }
		
		command = { type = local_setflag 	which = buildSpec }
	}
}

###############################################
# Trade Embargo Events
# Only embargoAxis, no particular need for embargoAllies
###############################################

#set embargos

event = {
	id = 2400200
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {	
		ai = yes
		local_flag = AllInit
		NOT = { local_flag = embargoAxis }
		OR = {
			war = { country = GER }
			AND = {
				country = USA
				OR = {
					flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG1
					flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG2
					flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG3
					flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG4
				}
			}
		}
		#Putting a delay on the Soviet embargo of Axis for game purposes
		#USSR may have trouble immediately finding replacement partners to sell resources to, resulting in insufficient supplies import and resource overstockpile limit losses.
		#It's best not to do this while it is facing a critical situation in 1941.
		#On the other hand, the European Axis tend to run out of oil way too quickly because they love to constantly move shit around, so another year or so of oil smuggling will do them good.
		OR = {
			NOT = { country = SOV }
			AND = {
				country = SOV
				event = { id = 2007041 days = 180 }
			}
		}
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		#Emargoing European neutrals as well to prevent re-exports
		command = { 																				type = embargo which = -1 where = GER value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { 				NOT = { war = { country = ITA country = GER } } }	type = embargo which = -1 where = ITA value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { 				NOT = { war = { country = SPA country = GER } } }	type = embargo which = -1 where = SPA value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { 				NOT = { war = { country = SPR country = GER } } }	type = embargo which = -1 where = SPR value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { 				NOT = { war = { country = POR country = GER } } }	type = embargo which = -1 where = POR value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { 				NOT = { war = { country = ROM country = GER } } }	type = embargo which = -1 where = ROM value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { 				NOT = { war = { country = HUN country = GER } } }	type = embargo which = -1 where = HUN value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { 				NOT = { war = { country = BUL country = GER } } }	type = embargo which = -1 where = BUL value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { 				NOT = { war = { country = YUG country = GER } } }	type = embargo which = -1 where = YUG value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { 				NOT = { war = { country = GRE country = GER } } }	type = embargo which = -1 where = GRE value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { 				NOT = { war = { country = TUR country = GER } } }	type = embargo which = -1 where = TUR value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { 				NOT = { war = { country = NOR country = GER } } }	type = embargo which = -1 where = NOR value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { 				NOT = { war = { country = SWE country = GER } } }	type = embargo which = -1 where = SWE value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { 				NOT = { war = { country = FIN country = GER } } }	type = embargo which = -1 where = FIN value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { 				NOT = { war = { country = CRO country = GER } } }	type = embargo which = -1 where = CRO value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { 				NOT = { war = { country = SER country = GER } } }	type = embargo which = -1 where = SER value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { 				NOT = { war = { country = SCH country = GER } } }	type = embargo which = -1 where = SCH value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { country = SOV	NOT = { war = { country = JAP country = GER } } }	type = embargo which = -1 where = JAP value = 1  }
		
		command = { trigger = { 				war = { country = ITA country = GER } }				type = embargo which = -1 where = ITA value = -1 }
		command = { trigger = { 				war = { country = SPA country = GER } }				type = embargo which = -1 where = SPA value = -1 }
		command = { trigger = { 				war = { country = SPR country = GER } }				type = embargo which = -1 where = SPR value = -1 }
		command = { trigger = { 				war = { country = POR country = GER } }				type = embargo which = -1 where = POR value = -1 }
		command = { trigger = { 				war = { country = ROM country = GER } }				type = embargo which = -1 where = ROM value = -1 }
		command = { trigger = { 				war = { country = HUN country = GER } }				type = embargo which = -1 where = HUN value = -1 }
		command = { trigger = { 				war = { country = BUL country = GER } }				type = embargo which = -1 where = BUL value = -1 }
		command = { trigger = { 				war = { country = YUG country = GER } }				type = embargo which = -1 where = YUG value = -1 }
		command = { trigger = { 				war = { country = GRE country = GER } }				type = embargo which = -1 where = GRE value = -1 }
		command = { trigger = { 				war = { country = TUR country = GER } }				type = embargo which = -1 where = TUR value = -1 }
		command = { trigger = { 				war = { country = NOR country = GER } }				type = embargo which = -1 where = NOR value = -1 }
		command = { trigger = { 				war = { country = SWE country = GER } }				type = embargo which = -1 where = SWE value = -1 }
		command = { trigger = { 				war = { country = FIN country = GER } }				type = embargo which = -1 where = FIN value = -1 }
		command = { trigger = { 				war = { country = CRO country = GER } }				type = embargo which = -1 where = CRO value = -1 }
		command = { trigger = { 				war = { country = SER country = GER } }				type = embargo which = -1 where = SER value = -1 }
		command = { trigger = { 				war = { country = SCH country = GER } }				type = embargo which = -1 where = SCH value = -1 }
		command = { trigger = { country = SOV	war = { country = JAP country = GER } }				type = embargo which = -1 where = JAP value = -1 }
		
		command = { type = local_setflag 	which = embargoAxis }
	}
}

#clear embargos

event = {
	id = 2400201
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {	
		ai = yes
		local_flag = AllInit
		local_flag = embargoAxis
		NOT = {
			war = { country = GER }
			#war = { country = JAP }
			AND = {
				country = USA
				OR = {
					flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG1
					flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG2
					flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG3
					flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG4
				}
			}
		}
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = embargo which = -1 where = GER value = -1 }
		command = { type = embargo which = -1 where = ITA value = -1 }
		command = { type = embargo which = -1 where = SPA value = -1 }
		command = { type = embargo which = -1 where = SPR value = -1 }
		command = { type = embargo which = -1 where = POR value = -1 }
		command = { type = embargo which = -1 where = ROM value = -1 }
		command = { type = embargo which = -1 where = HUN value = -1 }
		command = { type = embargo which = -1 where = BUL value = -1 }
		command = { type = embargo which = -1 where = YUG value = -1 }
		command = { type = embargo which = -1 where = GRE value = -1 }
		command = { type = embargo which = -1 where = TUR value = -1 }
		command = { type = embargo which = -1 where = NOR value = -1 }
		command = { type = embargo which = -1 where = SWE value = -1 }
		command = { type = embargo which = -1 where = FIN value = -1 }
		command = { type = embargo which = -1 where = CRO value = -1 }
		command = { type = embargo which = -1 where = SER value = -1 }
		command = { type = embargo which = -1 where = SCH value = -1 }
		command = { type = embargo which = -1 where = JAP value = -1 }
		
		command = { type = local_clrflag 	which = embargoAxis }
	}
}

#Embargo clear if one of the embargoed countries joined the allies

#to prevent the front AI from misbehaving, it must not be either allied or puppet of CHI and JAP
#currently using an alternative route through front_leader_home. Very risky but kind of cool.
event = { 
	id = 2401060
	random = no
	#invisible
	#country = MEN

	trigger = {
		country = MEN
		local_flag = AllInit
		ai = yes
		ai = JAP
		NOT = { 
			ai = CHI
			ai = U65
		}
		war = { country = U65 }
		NOT = { war = { country = JAP country = U65 } }
		puppet = { country = -1 country = JAP }
	}
	
	name = "AI_EVENT"
	style = 2
	
	#date = { year = 1936 month = january day = 1 }
	#offset = 5
	#deathdate = { year = 1938 month = january day = 3 }
	
	action = {
		command = { type = leave_alliance when = 0 }
		command = { type = end_puppet }

#Ger temporarily lower PanicRatio in response to invasions and rebellions

event = {
	id = 2401220
	random = no 
	country = GER
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {	
		ai = yes
		local_flag = AllInit
		year = 1942
		NOT = { local_flag = AI_Panic }
		#try to avoid rebels triggering this too...
		OR = {
			AND = {
				NOT = { local_flag = AI_Panic_Germany }
						region	= { region = "Germany" 		country = -2 	data = 1 value = 1  }
				NOT = { region	= { region = "Germany" 		country = REB 	data = 1 value = 1  } }
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { local_flag = AI_Panic_Italy }
						area	= { area = "South Italy"	country = -2 	data = 1 value = 1  }
				NOT = { area	= { area = "South Italy"	country = REB 	data = 1 value = 1  } }
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { local_flag = AI_Panic_Italy }
						area	= { area = "Central Italy"	country = -2 	data = 1 value = 1  }
				NOT = { area	= { area = "Central Italy"	country = REB 	data = 1 value = 1  } }
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { local_flag = AI_Panic_Italy }
						area	= { area = "North Italy"	country = -2 	data = 1 value = 1  }
				NOT = { area	= { area = "North Italy"	country = REB 	data = 1 value = 1  } }
			}
			#Ignore Sicily
			#AND = {
			#	NOT = { local_flag = AI_Panic_Sicily }
			#	area	= { area = "Sicilian Gap"	country = -2 data = 1 value = 40 }
			#}
			AND = {
				NOT = { local_flag = AI_Panic_France }
				event = { id = 2002004 days = 360 } #France fell
						region = { region = "France" 		country = -2 	data = 1 value = 1 }
				NOT = { region = { region = "France" 		country = REB	data = 1 value = 1 } }
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { local_flag = AI_Panic_Yugoslavia }
						war		= { country = U27 }
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { local_flag = AI_Panic_Greece }
						war		= { country = U33 }
			}
		}
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 5
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = { local_flag = AI_Panic_Germany }
								region	= { region = "Germany" 		country = -2 	data = 1 value = 1  }
						NOT = { region	= { region = "Germany" 		country = REB 	data = 1 value = 1  } }
					}
					type = local_setflag which = AI_Panic_Germany }
	
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = { local_flag = AI_Panic_Italy }
						OR = {
							AND = {
										area	= { area = "South Italy"	country = -2 	data = 1 value = 1  }
								NOT = { area	= { area = "South Italy"	country = REB 	data = 1 value = 1  } }
							}
							AND = {
										area	= { area = "Central Italy"	country = -2 	data = 1 value = 1  }
								NOT = { area	= { area = "Central Italy"	country = REB 	data = 1 value = 1  } }
							}
							AND = {
										area	= { area = "North Italy"	country = -2 	data = 1 value = 1  }
								NOT = { area	= { area = "North Italy"	country = REB 	data = 1 value = 1  } }
							}
						}
					}
					type = local_setflag which = AI_Panic_Italy }
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = { local_flag = AI_Panic_Sicily }
						area	= { area = "Sicilian Gap"	country = -2 data = 1 value = 40 }
					}
					type = local_setflag which = AI_Panic_Sicily }
					
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = { local_flag = AI_Panic_France }
						event = { id = 2002004 days = 360 } #France fell
								region = { region = "France" 		country = -2 	data = 1 value = 1 }
						NOT = { region = { region = "France" 		country = REB	data = 1 value = 1 } }
					}
					type = local_setflag which = AI_Panic_France }
					
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = { local_flag = AI_Panic_Yugoslavia }
						war		= { country = U27 }
					}
					type = local_setflag which = AI_Panic_Yugoslavia }
					
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = { local_flag = AI_Panic_Greece }
						war		= { country = U33 }
					}
					type = local_setflag which = AI_Panic_Greece }
	
		command = { type = event which = 2400036 where = GER when = 0  } #
		command = { type = event which = 2400037 where = GER when = 30 } #Reset to default in 1 month
	}
}

#Switch from mixed to naval
event = {
	id = 2401308
	random = no 
	country = JAP
	
	trigger = {	
		ai = yes
		local_flag = AllInit
		NOT = { war = { country = SOV } }
		OR = {
			year = 1939 #1 carrier takes over 3 years to build. Have to adjust down to 2
			war = { country = ENG }
			war = { country = USA }
			event = 550 #US Lend-Lease Act (Asia)
			flag = { which = japChi when = 1 value = 30  } #doing well in China
			war = { country = ENG country = GER }
		}
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }

#Start buildup for the pacific war
#stop new coastal invasions of china ? or continue until the coast is definitely secured?

event = {
	id = 2401310
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	country = JAP
	
	trigger = {	
		ai = yes
		local_flag = AllInit
		NOT = { local_flag = warPrep }
		NOT = {
			war = { country = SOV }
			war = { country = ENG }
			war = { country = USA }
		}
		flag = { which = japChi when = 1 value = 30 }
		#Total force size at least 40% of CHI's (including exp_forces)
		land_percentage = { country = CHI value = 0.40 }
		control = { province = 3086 data = -3 }
	}

	#name = "Japan War Prep"
	#desc = "Japan War Prep"
	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = ai 				which = "nations\JAP_prep.ai" }
		command = { type = local_setflag	which = warPrep }
		
		command = { type = remove_units which = all where = -2 when = 0 value = 3086 } #just in case
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 3086 } } } type = secedeprovince	which = JAP value = 3086 when = 2 } #just in case
	}
}

#China and Japan both de-escalate
#AI needs to stop trying to push into China and start preparing to face other threats, passivity is the only way to achieve that

event = {
	id = 2401330
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	tag = { JAP U87 CHI CHC U58 }
	
	trigger = {	
		local_flag = AllInit
		NOT = { local_flag = asiaPassive }
		ai = yes
		ai = JAP #china will only de-escalate if Japan does as well. Japan will try to de-escalate regardless
		NOT = { 
			war = { country = SOV country = JAP }
			war = { country = USA country = JAP }
			war = { country = ENG country = JAP }
		}
		war = { country = CHI country = JAP }
		NOT = { war = { country = U03 country = JAP } }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				flag = { which = japChi when = 1 value = 35  }
				control = { province = 1313 data = -3 }
			}
			flag = { which = japChi when = 1 value = 40  }
		}
		OR = {
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI2
			flag = SOV_Lend_Lease_to_CHI2
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI2
			AND = {
				flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI1
				flag = SOV_Lend_Lease_to_CHI1
			}
			AND = {
				flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI1
				flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI1
			}
			AND = {
				flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI1
				flag = SOV_Lend_Lease_to_CHI1
			}
		}
		#control = { province = 1235 data = -3 }
		#OR = {
		#	war = { country = SOV country = JAP }
		#	war = { country = ENG country = JAP }
		#	war = { country = USA country = JAP }
		#	event = 550 #US Lend-Lease Act (Asia)
		#	flag = { which = japChi when = 1 value = 50  } #doing well in China
		#	war = { country = ENG country = GER }
		#}
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	#name = "Asia Passive"
	#desc = "Asia Passive"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = local_setflag 	which = asiaPassive }
		command = { type = ai 				which = "nations\PassiveAsia.ai" }
	}
}

#Invade USSR if it has very few troops on the border

event = {
	id = 2401335
	random = no 
	country = JAP
	
	trigger = {	
		local_flag = AllInit
		NOT = { 
			war = { country = SOV }
			war = { country = GER }
		}
		war = { country = SOV country = GER }
		event = 2011004 #Japan anti-comintern pact
		OR = {
			NOT = { ai = SOV }
			NOT = { flag = JAP_SOV_NAP }
		}
		NOT = { lost_VP = { country = GER value = 5 } }
		NOT = { garrison = { country = SOV province = 1372 type = land size = 15 area = 2 } }
		NOT = { garrison = { country = SOV province = 1520 type = land size = 15 area = 2 } }
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		#+3 dissent if had a NAP, but it's worth it
		command = { type = clrflag which = JAP_SOV_NAP }
		command = { trigger = { non_aggression = { country = JAP country = SOV } } type = local_setflag which = dissentReduce5 when = 1 value = 1 }
		command = { type = end_non_aggression which = JAP where = SOV }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = dissentReduce5 when = 1 value = 1 } } type = dissent value = -2 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = dissentReduce5 when = 1 value = 1 } } type = local_clrflag which = dissentReduce5 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = dissentReduce5 when = 1 value = 1 } } type = belligerence value = 5 }
		command = { type = end_non_aggression which = MAN where = SOV }
		
		command = { type = war which = SOV }
	}
}

#Japan AI keeps losing its airforce to rebels... Even U87 garrisons are not sufficient to fight rebel threat, Japan itself has to garrison the airbases.

event = {
	id = 2401341
	random = no 
	country = JAP
	#persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {	
		ai = yes
		local_flag = AllInit
		militia = 2
		control = { province = 1205 data = -3 }
		area = { area = "Hebei" country = -3 data = 1 value = 80 }
		#NOT = { garrison = { country = JAP province = 1205 type = land size = 1 area = 0 } }
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = demobilize which = militia when = 0 where = 0 value = -2 }
		command = { type = activate_division which = 13016	value = 4001 when = 0 where = 1205  }
		command = { type = activate_division which = 13016	value = 4002 when = 0 where = 1205  }
		command = { type = disorg_division   which = 13016	value = 4001 where = -100 }
		command = { type = disorg_division   which = 13016	value = 4002 where = -100 }
	}
}

#Post-war demob
event = {
	id = 2401450
	random = no 
	#tag = { SOV CHC }
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {	
		ai = yes
		NOT = { local_flag = armyReduction }
		event = 2003202 #Potsdam
		policy = { type = professional_army value = 5 }
		NOT = { policy = { type = professional_army value = 6 } } #Removed General Mob
		NOT = { 
			war = { country = SOV }
			war = { country = ENG }
			war = { country = USA }
			war = { country = JAP }
			war = { country = CHI }
			war = { country = CHC }
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
		}
		OR = {
			AND = {
				NOT = {	country = CHI }
				NOT = {	country = CHC }
			}
			event = 2053023
			event = 2053033
		}
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = local_setflag which = armyReduction	}
		command = { type = dissent value = -1 }
		
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { country = CHI country = CHC } }	type = demobilize which = militia 	when = 0 where = 0 value =  50 }
		command = { trigger = { OR = { country = CHI country = CHC } }	type = demobilize which = militia 	when = 0 where = 0 value = 100 }
		command = { 													type = demobilize which = cavalry 	when = 0 where = 0 value =  20 }
		
		#keep disbanding infantry until there is at least some manpower reserve
		command = { 										type = strength 	which = all 	when = 1 where = 0 value =   1 } 
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { manpower = 200 } }	type = demobilize which = infantry	when = 0 where = 0 value = -10 }

#Berlin "blockade"
event = {
	id = 2401460
	random = no 
	country = SOV
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {	
		ai = yes
		event = { id = 2003202 days = 360 } #Potsdam
		NOT = { war = { country = USA }	}
		NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } }
		control = { province = 300	data = USA }
		control = { province = 7266	data = SOV }
		#access = { country = SOV country = USA }
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1944 }
	offset = 5
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = access which = USA }
		#days to resupply over air
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { ai = USA } } type = event which = 2401461 where = SOV when = 1 } #1 day
		command = { trigger = { 		ai = USA   } type = event which = 2401461 where = SOV when = 2 } #2 days
	}
}

event = {
	id = 2401461
	random = no 
	country = SOV
	persistent = yes

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	action = {
		command = { type = end_access which = USA when = 0 }
	}
}

#An unfortunate but necessary hack.
#Vichy has an inherent oil shortage (historically accurate), on top of that AI VIC keeps needlessly wasting precious oil on pointless air and naval maneuvers around the mediterranean
#German AI, none the wiser, insists on "helping" its puppet by sending massive amounts of oil to prop up the French economy.
#There are only two ways to prevent this behavior - turning off the auto-assistance to allies/puppets in general (would have horrible consequences)
#or putting a trade embargo between Germany and Vichy, which is also very undesirable.
#better alternative is just to give AI Vichy about as much free oil as it's going to be losing. (around -30 for civilian economy and -50 for military)
#on top of that, enforcing the "oil shortage" industrial modifier.

event = {
	id = 2401514
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	country = VIC
	
	trigger = {	
		local_flag = AllInit
		NOT = { local_flag = VICoil }
		puppet = { country = VIC country = GER }
		NOT = { alliance = { country = GER } }
		ai = VIC
		#ai = GER
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = industrial_modifier which = total	value = -15		} #oil shortage IC mod
		command = { type = free_oil 							value = 80  	}
		command = { type = local_setflag 						which = VICoil	}
	}
}

event = {
	id = 2401515
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	country = VIC
	
	trigger = {	
		local_flag = AllInit
		local_flag = VICoil
		NOT = {
			AND = {
				puppet = { country = VIC country = GER }
				NOT = { alliance = { country = GER } }
				ai = VIC
				#ai = GER
			}
		}
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = industrial_modifier which = total	value = 15		} #oil shortage IC mod
		command = { type = free_oil 							value = -80		}
		command = { type = local_clrflag 						which = VICoil	}
	}
}

	country = SPA

		ai = yes
		local_flag = AllInit
		war = { country = SPR }
		OR = {
			event = 2003011 #Treaty of Munich
			year = 1939
		}
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = ai which = "frontAInormal.ai" }
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = FrontAICautious }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = FrontAINormal }
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = FrontAIAggressive }
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = FrontAIVeryAggressive }
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = FrontAIHyper }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = FrontAILock }
		command = { type = ai which = "nations\SPA_1939.ai" }
		command = { type = ai which = "airLand.ai" }
	}
}

#Only enable invasion AI if fighting against human opponent. Unfortunately invasion AI is pretty reckless all things considered

event = {
	id = 2401603
	random = no 
	tag = { SPR SPA }
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {	
		ai = yes
		local_flag = AllInit
		NOT = { local_flag = InvOn }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				country = SPA
				OR = {
					AND = {
						war = { country = SPR }
						NOT = { ai = SPR }
					}
					AND = {
						war = { country = U52 }
						NOT = { ai = U52 }
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				country = SPR
				OR = {
					AND = {
						war = { country = SPA }
						NOT = { ai = SPA }
					}
					AND = {
						war = { country = U69 }
						NOT = { ai = U69 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = ai which = "invasionYes.ai" }

		local_flag = AllInit

			region = { region = "Norway" country = -2 data = 1 value = 60 }	#Losing in Norway

		}
	}

#Evacuate troops from France / Dunkirk

event = {
	id = 2401701
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {	
		NOT = { local_flag = noExpFRA }
		NOT = { local_flag = 2401701  }
		ai = yes
		local_flag = AllInit
		alliance = { country = ENG } #Includes england
		alliance = { country = VIC }
		NOT = { 
			country = VIC
			country = POL
			country = U36
			country = BEL
			country = HOL
		}
		#country = ENG #only england for now
		NOT = { event = 2002004 }
		NOT = { year = 1942 }
		NOT = { alliance = { country = USA } }
		war = { country = GER }
		NOT = { war = { country = GER country = SOV } }
		NOT = { region = { region = "Benelux" country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 } }	#Losing in Benelux
		OR = {
			control = { province = 56 data = -2 }		#Paris
			region = { region = "France" 	country = -2 data = 1 value = 50 } #Losing France in general
			AND = {
				NOT = { event = { id = 2003045 days = 180 } }
				OR = {
					control = { province = 41 	data = -2 }		#Le Havre
					control = { province = 7088	data = -2 }		#Abbeville
					control = { province = 18 	data = -2 }		#Calais
					area = { area = "Pas de Calais" country = -2 data = 1 value = 50 } #Losing Pas de Calais
				}
			}
		}
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 2 #Need to make a decision very quickly
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		trigger = { country = ENG }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = noExpFRA }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = noExpNOR }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = 2401701  }
		command = { 												type = ai which = "redeployDLow.ai" }
		command = { 												type = ai which = "redeploy90.ai"  }
		#command = { trigger = { NOT = { local_flag = noExpNOR } } 	type = ai which = "noExpFRA.ai"    }
		#command = { trigger = { 		local_flag = noExpNOR 	} 	type = ai which = "noExpNORFRA.ai" }
		command = { 												type = ai which = "noExpNORFRA.ai" } #if France is lost, always evacuate norway as well
		command = { 												type = ai which = "invasionNo.ai"  }
		command = { 												type = local_setflag which = InvShutOff }
		
		command = {								 					type = ai which = "nations\ENG_home.ai" } #home_2 oversea_0
		command = {  												type = ai which = "front_leader.ai" } #front_leader 200
		command = { 												type = event which = 2400040 where = VIC when = 0  } #exp_0
		command = { 												type = event which = 2400043 where = VIC when = 30 } #exp_30
		
		command = { 												type = event which = 2401703 where = ENG when = 18 } #front leader 30
		
		command = { 												type = add_corps 	value = naval where = 21 }
		command = { 												type = add_division value = transport when = 1 }
		command = { 												type = add_division value = transport when = 1 }
		command = { 												type = add_division value = transport when = 1 }
	}
	action = {
		trigger = { NOT = { country = ENG } }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = noExpFRA }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = noExpNOR }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = 2401701  }
		command = { 												type = ai which = "redeployDLow.ai" }
		command = { 												type = ai which = "redeploy90.ai"  }
		#command = { trigger = { NOT = { local_flag = noExpNOR } } 	type = ai which = "noExpFRA.ai"    }
		#command = { trigger = { 		local_flag = noExpNOR 	} 	type = ai which = "noExpNORFRA.ai" }
		command = { 												type = ai which = "noExpNORFRA.ai" } #if France is lost, always evacuate norway as well
		
		command = { trigger = { OR = { country = BEL country = HOL country = AST country = NZL country = SAF country = NZL country = U02 } } 	type = add_corps 	value = naval where = 21 }
		command = { trigger = { OR = { country = BEL country = HOL country = AST country = NZL country = SAF country = NZL country = U02 } } 	type = add_division value = transport when = 1 }
	}
}

		}

		NOT = { claims = { province = 56 } }

#####################################################################
#AI historic naval invasions
#####################################################################
#Norway
event = {
	id = 2405000
	random = no
	country = ENG

	name = "AI_EVENT"

	trigger = {
		ai = yes
		war = { country = GER }
		OR = {
			war = { country = NOR }
			AND = {
				alliance = { country = NOR }
				region = { region = "Norway" country = -2 value = 1 } #Germany is invading Norway
			}
		}
		#NOT = { garrison = { country = ENG province = 2276 type = land size = 1 area = 2 } } #English not present in Norway region
		OR = {
			garrison = { country = GER province = 2276 type = land size = 1 area = 2 } #Germans present
			event = 2001009 #Making first move
			puppet = { country = NOR country = GER }
		}
		NOT = { control = { province = 2276 data = GER } } #Narvik(+) held by Norway. either friend or enemy
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 3
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = january year = 1960 }

	action = {
		name = "AI_EVENT"
		command = { 										type = remove_division 					which = 12700 value = 5 }		#5th 'Globe Trotters' Division
		command = { trigger = { event = 2401860 }			type = remove_division 					which = 14800 value = 880 }		#5th 'Globe Trotters' Division
		command = { 										type = remove_division 					which = 12700 value = 1501 }	#49th 'West Riding' Division
		command = { 										type = remove_division 					which = 12700 value = 2 }		#1st Infantry Division
		command = { trigger = { event = 2401860 }			type = remove_division 					which = 14800 value = 885 }		#1st Infantry Division
		
		command = { type = ai which = "EXP_15.ai" }
		
		#Adrian Carton de Wiart (10053) / Pierse Joseph Mackesy (?) Claude Auchinleck (10012) / Bernard Charles Tolver Paget (10200)
		command = { type = add_corps which = "Mauriceforce Expeditionary Force" value = land when = 10053 where = 2276 }  # Wiart
		command = { type = activate_division which = 12700 value = 10020 where = 2276 when = 1 } #5th 'Globe Trotters' Division
		command = { type = add_corps which = "Sickleforce Expeditionary Force" value = land when = 10200 where = 2276 }  # Paget
		command = { type = activate_division which = 12700 value = 10021 where = 2276 when = 1 } #49th 'West Riding' Division
		command = { type = add_corps which = "Rupertforce Expeditionary Force" value = land when = 10012 where = 2276 } # Mackesy
		command = { type = activate_division which = 12700 value = 10022 where = 2276 when = 1 } #1st Infantry Division

event = { 
	id = 2405040
	random = no 
	country = USA
	save_date = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = yes
		ai = ENG
		NOT 		= { local_flag = war_preparedness_debuff }
		alliance 	= { country = ENG }
		war 		= { country = GER }
		war			= { country = GER country = SOV }
		NOT 		= { flag = ENG_invaded }
		event = { id = 2002004 days = 360 } #1 year since the defeat of france
		NOT = { region = { region = "Egypt" 	country = -2 value = 25 } }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Libya" country = -3 value = 80 } #Advancing across Libya
				event = { id = 2002004 days = 1260 } #3.5 years since the defeat of france
			}
			control = { province = 515 data = -3 } #Rome
			NOT = { control = { province = 485 data = -2 } } #Warsaw
			NOT = { control = { province = 510 data = -2 } } #Konigsberg
		}
		
		#Need at least 50 days from the Dragoon invasion to rebase the transports. The allies originally planned to launch both operations at the same time, but realized that they don't have enough landing craft
		OR = {
			event = { id = 2405050 days = 50 }
			NOT = { event = 2405050 }
		}
		
		month = 2
		NOT = { month = 10 }
		
		OR = {
			ai = GER
			AND = {
				area = { area = "Normandy" 		country = -2 value = 20 }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 39 		type = land size = 12 area = 1 } }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 39 		type = land size = 4  area = 0 } }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 3160		type = land size = 4  area = 0 } }
			}
			AND = {
				area = { area = "Brittany" 		country = -2 value = 20 }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 32		type = land size = 12 area = 1 } }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 32		type = land size = 4  area = 0 } }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 3158		type = land size = 4  area = 0 } }
			}
			AND = {
				area = { area = "Pas de Calais" country = -2 value = 20 }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 18		type = land size = 12 area = 1 } }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 18		type = land size = 4  area = 0 } }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 43		type = land size = 4  area = 0 } }
			}
			#AND = {
			#	area = { area = "Netherlands" 	country = -2 value = 20 }
			#	NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 47		type = land size = 12 area = 1 } }
			#	NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 47		type = land size = 4  area = 0 } }
			#	NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 46		type = land size = 4  area = 0 } }
			#}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = january year = 1960 }
	
	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	action = {
		ai_chance = 80
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				ai = GER
				AND = {
					area = { area = "Normandy" 		country = -2 value = 20 }
					NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 39 		type = land size = 12 area = 1 } }
					NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 39 		type = land size = 4  area = 0 } }
					NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 3160		type = land size = 4  area = 0 } }
				}
			}
		}
		command = { type = setflag which = overlordNormandy }
		
		command = { type = addclaim which = 39   where = ENG  }
		command = { type = addclaim which = 3160 where = USA  }
		
		command = { type = event which = 2405041 where = USA when = 0 }	
	}
	action = {
		ai_chance = 10
		trigger = {
			area = { area = "Brittany" 		country = -2 value = 20 }
			NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 32		type = land size = 12 area = 1 } }
			NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 32		type = land size = 4  area = 0 } }

		#To prevent early revert to France
		#command = { type = add_claim_region which = "France" where = USA }
		
		#Problem: All historical leaders start the game as Mj Generals, and there is no way to tell which ones will end up auto-promoted
		#Solution: Leaving the HQ without a general as the AI is able to assign a leader to it in the middle of battle after 24 hours

#USA regulate aggressiveness in order to reduce casualty rate. Can't afford to waste manpower like China does

event = { 
	id = 2405171
	random = no 
	tag = { USA }
	persistent = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = yes
		war	= { country = PRK }
		event = 2049624
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1944 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = january year = 1965 }
	
	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	action = {
		trigger = { 
				NOT = { local_flag = FrontAICautious }
				NOT = { war = { country = SOV } }
				control = { province = 1199	data = -3 }
				control = { province = 1198	data = -3 }
				control = { province = 3100	data = -3 }
		}
		command = { type = ai which = "nations\PassiveAsia.ai"	  		}
		command = { type = ai which = "frontAIcautious.ai" 				}
		command = { type = local_setflag which = FrontAILock			}
		command = { type = local_setflag which = FrontAICautious		}
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = FrontAINormal			}
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = FrontAIAggressive		}
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = FrontAIVeryAggressive	}
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = FrontAIHyper			}
	}
	action = {
		trigger = { 
			local_flag = FrontAICautious
			OR = {
				war = { country = SOV }
				NOT = { control = { province = 1199	data = -3 } }
				NOT = { control = { province = 1198	data = -3 } }
				NOT = { control = { province = 3100	data = -3 } }
			}
		}
		command = { type = ai which = "nations\PassiveDisable.ai"  		}
		command = { type = ai which = "frontAIaggressive.ai"			}
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = FrontAILock			}
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = FrontAICautious		}
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = FrontAINormal			}
		command = { type = local_setflag which = FrontAIAggressive		}
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = FrontAIVeryAggressive	}
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = FrontAIHyper			}
	}
}
